# Graph Report - autonomous-ai-agency  (2026-10-15)

## Corpus Check
- 1784 files · ~2,591,620 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 57 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 17, .bat 5, .css 5)

## Summary
- 38132 nodes · 80254 edges · 1495 communities (1276 shown, 219 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 90% EXTRACTED · 10% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7786 edges (avg confidence: 0.93)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `3816b742`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
